{"parentUuid":"22540ade-937a-4331-9be3-51b3492bb065","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"39f27084-b50e-473e-be26-800a00cbc827","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VfoEBPQ7zPD1kgpA3JLex2","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t#!/usr/bin/env bash\n2\t# Firing-test for qa-pending-auto-mover.sh (Phase 3.5 T7 retrofit; S61).\n3\t#\n4\t# Hook purpose (HH-E.2 / D-031 Stop): scan human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/\n5\t# for bundles whose frontmatter `status:` starts with answered-|closed-|\n6\t# resolved-, AND whose `wait_until:` ISO is past (or absent), AND no global\n7\t# `.auto-mv-paused` kill switch. Mv qualifying bundles to answered/.\n8\t# Idempotent per session via marker file.\n9\t#\n10\t# Test strategy: stage temp PROJECT_DIR with various pending bundle fixtures;\n11\t# invoke hook; assert mv outcome (file moved or not) + log entries.\n12\t#\n13\t# 6 test cases:\n14\t#   TC1 — pending dir missing → silent + marker touched\n15\t#   TC2 — kill switch (.auto-mv-paused) present → skip all + log\n16\t#   TC3 — bundle status=answered-via-chat, no wait_until → MV to answered/\n17\t#   TC4 — bundle status=open → SKIP (wrong status)\n18\t#   TC5 — bundle status=answered + wait_until in future → DEFER\n19\t#   TC6 — idempotent (marker prevents 2nd-call re-fire)\n20\t#\n21\t# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\n22\tset -euo pipefail\n23\t\n24\tSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\n25\tHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../qa-pending-auto-mover.sh\"\n26\t[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n27\t\n28\tTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\n29\ttrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n30\t\n31\tLOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.qa-auto-mv.log\"\n32\tPEND=\"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/pending\"\n33\tANS=\"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered\"\n34\tKILL_SWITCH=\"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/.auto-mv-paused\"\n35\t\n36\trun_hook() {\n37\t  local sid=\"${1:-tc-session}\"\n38\t  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" CLAUDE_SESSION_ID=\"$sid\" \\\n39\t    bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n40\t}\n41\t\n42\tclean_state() {\n43\t  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace\"\n44\t  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\" \"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/q-and-a\"\n45\t}\n46\t\n47\tmarker_path() {\n48\t  echo \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.qa-auto-mv-fired-$1\"\n49\t}\n50\t\n51\t# --- TC1: pending dir missing → silent + marker touched ---\n52\tclean_state\n53\trun_hook \"tc1\"\n54\tif [ ! -f \"$(marker_path tc1)\" ]; then\n55\t  echo \"FAIL TC1: marker should be touched even when pending/ missing\"\n56\t  exit 1\n57\tfi\n58\techo \"PASS TC1: pending/ missing → silent + marker touched\"\n59\t\n60\t# --- TC2: kill switch present → skip all + log ---\n61\tclean_state\n62\tmkdir -p \"$PEND\"\n63\t: > \"$KILL_SWITCH\"\n64\tcat > \"$PEND/bundle1.md\" <<'EOF'\n65\t---\n66\tstatus: answered-via-chat\n67\t---\n68\tcontent\n69\tEOF\n70\trun_hook \"tc2\"\n71\tif [ ! -f \"$PEND/bundle1.md\" ]; then\n72\t  echo \"FAIL TC2: kill switch should prevent mv; bundle was moved\"\n73\t  exit 1\n74\tfi\n75\tif ! grep -q \"kill switch\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n76\t  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected 'kill switch' in log\"\n77\t  cat \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || echo \"(no log)\"\n78\t  exit 1\n79\tfi\n80\techo \"PASS TC2: kill switch → skip all + log\"\n81\t\n82\t# --- TC3: status=answered-via-chat, no wait_until → MV ---\n83\tclean_state\n84\tmkdir -p \"$PEND\"\n85\tcat > \"$PEND/bundle-tc3.md\" <<'EOF'\n86\t---\n87\tstatus: answered-via-chat\n88\t---\n89\tContent here.\n90\tEOF\n91\trun_hook \"tc3\"\n92\tif [ -f \"$PEND/bundle-tc3.md\" ]; then\n93\t  echo \"FAIL TC3: bundle should have been moved to answered/\"\n94\t  ls -la \"$PEND\" \"$ANS\" 2>&1\n95\t  cat \"$LOG\" 2>&1\n96\t  exit 1\n97\tfi\n98\tif [ ! -f \"$ANS/bundle-tc3.md\" ]; then\n99\t  echo \"FAIL TC3: bundle should appear in answered/\"\n100\t  ls -la \"$ANS\" 2>&1\n101\t  exit 1\n102\tfi\n103\tif ! grep -q \"MV bundle-tc3\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n104\t  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected MV log entry\"\n105\t  cat \"$LOG\"\n106\t  exit 1\n107\tfi\n108\techo \"PASS TC3: status=answered-via-chat → MV to answered/\"\n109\t\n110\t# --- TC4: status=open → SKIP ---\n111\tclean_state\n112\tmkdir -p \"$PEND\"\n113\tcat > \"$PEND/bundle-tc4.md\" <<'EOF'\n114\t---\n115\tstatus: open\n116\t---\n117\tOpen question.\n118\tEOF\n119\trun_hook \"tc4\"\n120\tif [ ! -f \"$PEND/bundle-tc4.md\" ]; then\n121\t  echo \"FAIL TC4: open bundle should NOT be moved\"\n122\t  exit 1\n123\tfi\n124\tif ! grep -q \"SKIP bundle-tc4\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n125\t  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected SKIP log entry\"\n126\t  cat \"$LOG\"\n127\t  exit 1\n128\tfi\n129\techo \"PASS TC4: status=open → SKIP (no mv)\"\n130\t\n131\t# --- TC5: status=answered + wait_until in future → DEFER ---\n132\tclean_state\n133\tmkdir -p \"$PEND\"\n134\tFUTURE_TS=\"$(date -d '+1 hour' -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date -u -v+1H +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S 2>/dev/null || echo \"2099-01-01T00:00:00Z\")\"\n135\tcat > \"$PEND/bundle-tc5.md\" <<EOF\n136\t---\n137\tstatus: answered-via-chat\n138\twait_until: $FUTURE_TS\n139\t---\n140\tWait until future.\n141\tEOF\n142\trun_hook \"tc5\"\n143\tif [ ! -f \"$PEND/bundle-tc5.md\" ]; then\n144\t  echo \"FAIL TC5: bundle with future wait_until should be deferred (not moved)\"\n145\t  exit 1\n146\tfi\n147\tif ! grep -q \"DEFER bundle-tc5\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n148\t  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected DEFER log entry\"\n149\t  cat \"$LOG\"\n150\t  exit 1\n151\tfi\n152\techo \"PASS TC5: wait_until in future → DEFER\"\n153\t\n154\t# --- TC6: idempotent (marker prevents 2nd-call re-fire) ---\n155\tclean_state\n156\tmkdir -p \"$PEND\"\n157\tcat > \"$PEND/bundle-tc6.md\" <<'EOF'\n158\t---\n159\tstatus: answered-via-chat\n160\t---\n161\tRound 1.\n162\tEOF\n163\trun_hook \"tc6\"\n164\tLOG_LINES_1=$(wc -l < \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')\n165\t[ -z \"$LOG_LINES_1\" ] && LOG_LINES_1=0\n166\t# Stage another bundle; second invocation must NOT process it (marker exists)\n167\tcat > \"$PEND/bundle-tc6-second.md\" <<'EOF'\n168\t---\n169\tstatus: answered-via-chat\n170\t---\n171\tRound 2.\n172\tEOF\n173\trun_hook \"tc6\"\n174\tLOG_LINES_2=$(wc -l < \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')\n175\t[ -z \"$LOG_LINES_2\" ] && LOG_LINES_2=0\n176\tif [ \"$LOG_LINES_1\" != \"$LOG_LINES_2\" ]; then\n177\t  echo \"FAIL TC6: idempotency broken — second call wrote new log lines (1=$LOG_LINES_1 2=$LOG_LINES_2)\"\n178\t  cat \"$LOG\"\n179\t  exit 1\n180\tfi\n181\tif [ ! -f \"$PEND/bundle-tc6-second.md\" ]; then\n182\t  echo \"FAIL TC6: second bundle should NOT be moved (marker prevents 2nd-call)\"\n183\t  exit 1\n184\tfi\n185\techo \"PASS TC6: idempotent — marker prevents second-call re-fire\"\n186\t\n187\techo \"\"\n188\techo \"=== ALL FIRING-TESTS PASSED (6/6) ===\"\n189\techo \"qa-pending-auto-mover.sh externally-observable behavior verified.\"\n190\texit 0\n191\t"}]},"uuid":"7c3f5339-4904-43a9-8244-e80cddc97c4b","timestamp":"2026-05-06T13:49:35.953Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\firing-tests\\qa-pending-auto-mover-fire-test.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Firing-test for qa-pending-auto-mover.sh (Phase 3.5 T7 retrofit; S61).\n#\n# Hook purpose (HH-E.2 / D-031 Stop): scan human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/\n# for bundles whose frontmatter `status:` starts with answered-|closed-|\n# resolved-, AND whose `wait_until:` ISO is past (or absent), AND no global\n# `.auto-mv-paused` kill switch. Mv qualifying bundles to answered/.\n# Idempotent per session via marker file.\n#\n# Test strategy: stage temp PROJECT_DIR with various pending bundle fixtures;\n# invoke hook; assert mv outcome (file moved or not) + log entries.\n#\n# 6 test cases:\n#   TC1 — pending dir missing → silent + marker touched\n#   TC2 — kill switch (.auto-mv-paused) present → skip all + log\n#   TC3 — bundle status=answered-via-chat, no wait_until → MV to answered/\n#   TC4 — bundle status=open → SKIP (wrong status)\n#   TC5 — bundle status=answered + wait_until in future → DEFER\n#   TC6 — idempotent (marker prevents 2nd-call re-fire)\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../qa-pending-auto-mover.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nLOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.qa-auto-mv.log\"\nPEND=\"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/pending\"\nANS=\"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered\"\nKILL_SWITCH=\"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/.auto-mv-paused\"\n\nrun_hook() {\n  local sid=\"${1:-tc-session}\"\n  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" CLAUDE_SESSION_ID=\"$sid\" \\\n    bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n}\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\" \"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/q-and-a\"\n}\n\nmarker_path() {\n  echo \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.qa-auto-mv-fired-$1\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: pending dir missing → silent + marker touched ---\nclean_state\nrun_hook \"tc1\"\nif [ ! -f \"$(marker_path tc1)\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: marker should be touched even when pending/ missing\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: pending/ missing → silent + marker touched\"\n\n# --- TC2: kill switch present → skip all + log ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$PEND\"\n: > \"$KILL_SWITCH\"\ncat > \"$PEND/bundle1.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nstatus: answered-via-chat\n---\ncontent\nEOF\nrun_hook \"tc2\"\nif [ ! -f \"$PEND/bundle1.md\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: kill switch should prevent mv; bundle was moved\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"kill switch\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected 'kill switch' in log\"\n  cat \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || echo \"(no log)\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: kill switch → skip all + log\"\n\n# --- TC3: status=answered-via-chat, no wait_until → MV ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$PEND\"\ncat > \"$PEND/bundle-tc3.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nstatus: answered-via-chat\n---\nContent here.\nEOF\nrun_hook \"tc3\"\nif [ -f \"$PEND/bundle-tc3.md\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: bundle should have been moved to answered/\"\n  ls -la \"$PEND\" \"$ANS\" 2>&1\n  cat \"$LOG\" 2>&1\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ ! -f \"$ANS/bundle-tc3.md\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: bundle should appear in answered/\"\n  ls -la \"$ANS\" 2>&1\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"MV bundle-tc3\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected MV log entry\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: status=answered-via-chat → MV to answered/\"\n\n# --- TC4: status=open → SKIP ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$PEND\"\ncat > \"$PEND/bundle-tc4.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nstatus: open\n---\nOpen question.\nEOF\nrun_hook \"tc4\"\nif [ ! -f \"$PEND/bundle-tc4.md\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: open bundle should NOT be moved\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"SKIP bundle-tc4\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected SKIP log entry\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC4: status=open → SKIP (no mv)\"\n\n# --- TC5: status=answered + wait_until in future → DEFER ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$PEND\"\nFUTURE_TS=\"$(date -d '+1 hour' -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date -u -v+1H +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S 2>/dev/null || echo \"2099-01-01T00:00:00Z\")\"\ncat > \"$PEND/bundle-tc5.md\" <<EOF\n---\nstatus: answered-via-chat\nwait_until: $FUTURE_TS\n---\nWait until future.\nEOF\nrun_hook \"tc5\"\nif [ ! -f \"$PEND/bundle-tc5.md\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: bundle with future wait_until should be deferred (not moved)\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"DEFER bundle-tc5\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected DEFER log entry\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC5: wait_until in future → DEFER\"\n\n# --- TC6: idempotent (marker prevents 2nd-call re-fire) ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$PEND\"\ncat > \"$PEND/bundle-tc6.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nstatus: answered-via-chat\n---\nRound 1.\nEOF\nrun_hook \"tc6\"\nLOG_LINES_1=$(wc -l < \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')\n[ -z \"$LOG_LINES_1\" ] && LOG_LINES_1=0\n# Stage another bundle; second invocation must NOT process it (marker exists)\ncat > \"$PEND/bundle-tc6-second.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nstatus: answered-via-chat\n---\nRound 2.\nEOF\nrun_hook \"tc6\"\nLOG_LINES_2=$(wc -l < \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')\n[ -z \"$LOG_LINES_2\" ] && LOG_LINES_2=0\nif [ \"$LOG_LINES_1\" != \"$LOG_LINES_2\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: idempotency broken — second call wrote new log lines (1=$LOG_LINES_1 2=$LOG_LINES_2)\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ ! -f \"$PEND/bundle-tc6-second.md\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: second bundle should NOT be moved (marker prevents 2nd-call)\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC6: idempotent — marker prevents second-call re-fire\"\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== ALL FIRING-TESTS PASSED (6/6) ===\"\necho \"qa-pending-auto-mover.sh externally-observable behavior verified.\"\nexit 0\n","numLines":191,"startLine":1,"totalLines":191}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"22540ade-937a-4331-9be3-51b3492bb065","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"a66c838a-2292-4d0c-8968-e81af473cb00","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Mv qualifying bundles to answered/.\n# Idempotent per HOUR-BUCKET via marker file (L-S108-1; was per-session\n# but $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID empty on Windows → fallback-to-constant lockout).\n#\n# Test strategy: stage temp PROJECT_DIR with various pending bundle fixtures;\n# invoke hook; assert mv outcome (file moved or not) + log entries.\n#\n# 7 test cases:\n#   TC1 — pending dir missing → silent + current-bucket marker created\n#   TC2 — kill switch (.auto-mv-paused) present → skip all + log\n#   TC3 — bundle status=answered-via-chat, no wait_until → MV to answered/\n#   TC4 — bundle status=open → SKIP (wrong status)\n#   TC5 — bundle status=answered + wait_until in future → DEFER\n#   TC6 — idempotent (marker prevents 2nd-call re-fire same hour)\n#   TC7 — L-S108-1 REGRESSION: stale cross-bucket markers + legacy \"main\"\n#         fallback marker present → cleanup deletes both, MV happens\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Firing-test for qa-pending-auto-mover.sh (Phase 3.5 T7 retrofit; S61).\n#\n# Hook purpose (HH-E.2 / D-031 Stop): scan human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/\n# for bundles whose frontmatter `status:` starts with answered-|closed-|\n# resolved-, AND whose `wait_until:` ISO is past (or absent), AND no global\n# `.auto-mv-paused` kill switch. Mv qualifying bundles to answered/.\n# Idempotent per session via marker file.\n#\n# Test strategy: stage temp PROJECT_DIR with various pending bundle fixtures;\n# invoke hook; assert mv outcome (file moved or not) + log entries.\n#\n# 6 test cases:\n#   TC1 — pending dir missing → silent + marker touched\n#   TC2 — kill switch (.auto-mv-paused) present → skip all + log\n#   TC3 — bundle status=answered-via-chat, no wait_until → MV to answered/\n#   TC4 — bundle status=open → SKIP (wrong status)\n#   TC5 — bundle status=answered + wait_until in future → DEFER\n#   TC6 — idempotent (marker prevents 2nd-call re-fire)\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../qa-pending-auto-mover.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nLOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.qa-auto-mv.log\"\nPEND=\"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/pending\"\nANS=\"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered\"\nKILL_SWITCH=\"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/.auto-mv-paused\"\n\nrun_hook() {\n  local sid=\"${1:-tc-session}\"\n  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" CLAUDE_SESSION_ID=\"$sid\" \\\n    bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n}\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\" \"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/q-and-a\"\n}\n\nmarker_path() {\n  echo \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.qa-auto-mv-fired-$1\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: pending dir missing → silent + marker touched ---\nclean_state\nrun_hook \"tc1\"\nif [ ! -f \"$(marker_path tc1)\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: marker should be touched even when pending/ missing\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: pending/ missing → silent + marker touched\"\n\n# --- TC2: kill switch present → skip all + log ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$PEND\"\n: > \"$KILL_SWITCH\"\ncat > \"$PEND/bundle1.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nstatus: answered-via-chat\n---\ncontent\nEOF\nrun_hook \"tc2\"\nif [ ! -f \"$(current_marker)\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC7: current-bucket marker should be created post-MV\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC7: L-S108-1 REGRESSION — stale markers cleaned + bundle MV'd\"\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== ALL FIRING-TESTS PASSED (7/7) ===\"\necho \"qa-pending-auto-mover.sh externally-observable behavior verified.\"\necho \"L-S108-1 stale-marker regression COVERED (TC7).\"\nexit 0","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Firing-test for qa-pending-auto-mover.sh (Phase 3.5 T7 retrofit; S61).\n# Extended at S108 with TC7 — L-S108-1 stale-marker regression test.\n#\n# Hook purpose (HH-E.2 / D-031 Stop): scan human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/\n# for bundles whose frontmatter `status:` starts with answered-|closed-|\n# resolved-, AND whose `wait_until:` ISO is past (or absent), AND no global\n# `.auto-mv-paused` kill switch. Mv qualifying bundles to answered/.\n# Idempotent per HOUR-BUCKET via marker file (L-S108-1; was per-session\n# but $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID empty on Windows → fallback-to-constant lockout).\n#\n# Test strategy: stage temp PROJECT_DIR with various pending bundle fixtures;\n# invoke hook; assert mv outcome (file moved or not) + log entries.\n#\n# 7 test cases:\n#   TC1 — pending dir missing → silent + current-bucket marker created\n#   TC2 — kill switch (.auto-mv-paused) present → skip all + log\n#   TC3 — bundle status=answered-via-chat, no wait_until → MV to answered/\n#   TC4 — bundle status=open → SKIP (wrong status)\n#   TC5 — bundle status=answered + wait_until in future → DEFER\n#   TC6 — idempotent (marker prevents 2nd-call re-fire same hour)\n#   TC7 — L-S108-1 REGRESSION: stale cross-bucket markers + legacy \"main\"\n#         fallback marker present → cleanup deletes both, MV happens\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../qa-pending-auto-mover.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nLOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.qa-auto-mv.log\"\nPEND=\"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/pending\"\nANS=\"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered\"\nKILL_SWITCH=\"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/.auto-mv-paused\"\n\nrun_hook() {\n  local sid=\"${1:-tc-session}\"\n  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" CLAUDE_SESSION_ID=\"$sid\" \\\n    bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n}\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\" \"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/q-and-a\"\n}\n\ncurrent_marker() {\n  # Hour-bucket marker per L-S108-1 (matches hook's date +%Y%m%d-%H)\n  echo \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.qa-auto-mv-fired-$(date +%Y%m%d-%H)\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: pending dir missing → silent + current-bucket marker touched ---\nclean_state\nrun_hook \"tc1\"\nif [ ! -f \"$(current_marker)\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: current-bucket marker should be touched even when pending/ missing\"\n  ls -la \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/\" 2>&1\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: pending/ missing → silent + current-bucket marker touched\"\n\n# --- TC2: kill switch present → skip all + log ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$PEND\"\n: > \"$KILL_SWITCH\"\ncat > \"$PEND/bundle1.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nstatus: answered-via-chat\n---\ncontent\nEOF\nrun_hook \"tc2\"\nif [ ! -f \"$PEND/bundle1.md\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: kill switch should prevent mv; bundle was moved\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"kill switch\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected 'kill switch' in log\"\n  cat \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || echo \"(no log)\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: kill switch → skip all + log\"\n\n# --- TC3: status=answered-via-chat, no wait_until → MV ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$PEND\"\ncat > \"$PEND/bundle-tc3.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nstatus: answered-via-chat\n---\nContent here.\nEOF\nrun_hook \"tc3\"\nif [ -f \"$PEND/bundle-tc3.md\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: bundle should have been moved to answered/\"\n  ls -la \"$PEND\" \"$ANS\" 2>&1\n  cat \"$LOG\" 2>&1\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ ! -f \"$ANS/bundle-tc3.md\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: bundle should appear in answered/\"\n  ls -la \"$ANS\" 2>&1\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"MV bundle-tc3\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected MV log entry\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: status=answered-via-chat → MV to answered/\"\n\n# --- TC4: status=open → SKIP ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$PEND\"\ncat > \"$PEND/bundle-tc4.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nstatus: open\n---\nOpen question.\nEOF\nrun_hook \"tc4\"\nif [ ! -f \"$PEND/bundle-tc4.md\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: open bundle should NOT be moved\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"SKIP bundle-tc4\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected SKIP log entry\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC4: status=open → SKIP (no mv)\"\n\n# --- TC5: status=answered + wait_until in future → DEFER ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$PEND\"\nFUTURE_TS=\"$(date -d '+1 hour' -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date -u -v+1H +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S 2>/dev/null || echo \"2099-01-01T00:00:00Z\")\"\ncat > \"$PEND/bundle-tc5.md\" <<EOF\n---\nstatus: answered-via-chat\nwait_until: $FUTURE_TS\n---\nWait until future.\nEOF\nrun_hook \"tc5\"\nif [ ! -f \"$PEND/bundle-tc5.md\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: bundle with future wait_until should be deferred (not moved)\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"DEFER bundle-tc5\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected DEFER log entry\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC5: wait_until in future → DEFER\"\n\n# --- TC6: idempotent (marker prevents 2nd-call re-fire) ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$PEND\"\ncat > \"$PEND/bundle-tc6.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nstatus: answered-via-chat\n---\nRound 1.\nEOF\nrun_hook \"tc6\"\nLOG_LINES_1=$(wc -l < \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')\n[ -z \"$LOG_LINES_1\" ] && LOG_LINES_1=0\n# Stage another bundle; second invocation must NOT process it (marker exists)\ncat > \"$PEND/bundle-tc6-second.md\" <<'EOF'\n---\nstatus: answered-via-chat\n---\nRound 2.\nEOF\nrun_hook \"tc6\"\nLOG_LINES_2=$(wc -l < \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')\n[ -z \"$LOG_LINES_2\" ] && LOG_LINES_2=0\nif [ \"$LOG_LINES_1\" != \"$LOG_LINES_2\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: idempotency broken — second call wrote new log lines (1=$LOG_LINES_1 2=$LOG_LINES_2)\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ ! -f \"$PEND/bundle-tc6-second.md\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: second bundle should NOT be moved (marker prevents 2nd-call)\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC6: idempotent — marker prevents second-call re-fire\"\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== ALL FIRING-TESTS PASSED (6/6) ===\"\necho \"qa-pending-auto-mover.sh externally-observable behavior verified.\"\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":190,"oldLines":7,"newStart":190,"newLines":49,"lines":[" fi"," echo \"PASS TC6: idempotent — marker prevents second-call re-fire\""," ","+# --- TC7: L-S108-1 REGRESSION — stale cross-bucket marker + legacy fallback ---","+# Reproduces M-S108-1 bug: agent stuck for 24+h because .qa-auto-mv-fired-main","+# (legacy CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-main fallback marker) blocked all subsequent","+# sessions. {"parentUuid":"e5e91681-6cec-4a43-a985-192a051ea83e","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"39f27084-b50e-473e-be26-800a00cbc827","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019MT9CNaEcHjmzT86hrRq1g","type":"tool_result","content":"155\tfi\n156\t\n157\t# === Check 6: L-S48m-1 CLAUDE_SESSION_ID empty-on-Windows anti-pattern (Pattern B) ===\n158\t# Origin: S48m profile-template-auto-populate.sh — marker filename used $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID\n159\t# without :- fallback; on Windows the env var is empty → marker collides on every fire →\n160\t# silent skip after first fire. L-S48m-1 fix: use session-log basename instead.\n161\tif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n162\t  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n163\t    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n164\t    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n165\t    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n166\t    BAD_B=\"$(grep -nE \"[\\\"]?\\\\.[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+(-fired|-marker|-ran|-flag)-\\\\\\$\\\\{?CLAUDE_SESSION_ID\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -vE \"^[[:space:]]*[0-9]+:[[:space:]]*#\" || true)\"\n167\t    if [ -n \"$BAD_B\" ]; then\n168\t      emit \"L-S48m-1-CLAUDE-SESSION-ID-MARKER\" \"$bn\" \"marker filename uses \\$CLAUDE_SESSION_ID (empty on Windows; silent skip — L-S48m-1)\"\n169\t    fi\n170\t  done\n171\tfi\n172\t\n173\t# === Check 7: L-S48d-1 pipefail+ERR-trap+bare-grep anti-pattern (Pattern C) — REFINED S58 ===\n174\t# Origin: S48d profile-template-auto-populate.sh — set -uo pipefail + trap 'exit 0' ERR\n175\t# + bare `grep -m1 ...` returning nonzero on no-match → ERR trap fires silently → script\n176\t# exits 0 with NO log line → invisible failure. Fix: wrap each grep with `|| true` or\n177\t# convert to `if grep ...; then ... fi` form.\n178\t#\n179\t# S54 refinement: caught command-sub + pipeline forms (was bare-line only).\n180\t#\n181\t# S58 refinement (closes KI-S54-1): replaces the 3-pass grep filter with an awk script\n182\t# that tracks pipefail-bracket state + multi-line `\\`-continuation joining + 4 NEW\n183\t# false-positive recognitions surfaced by S57 ratify-via-comment categorization sample:\n184\t#\n185\t#   NEW skip rules:\n186\t#     (1) Compound conditional `if X && grep`/`if X | grep`/`elif X && grep`/etc — line\n187\t#         starts with `if|while|until|elif` AND contains `grep` (cond exit consumed by `if`)\n188\t#     (2) Alt-guard `|| echo NN`/`|| echo \"\"`/`|| echo` end-of-pipeline (substitute\n189\t#         non-zero with echo output → command-sub/pipeline returns 0 → ERR trap exempt)\n190\t#     (3) Alt-guard `|| exit N`/`|| return N` (explicit exit handling)\n191\t#     (4) Pipefail-bracket `set +o pipefail; grep; set -o pipefail` (pipefail temporarily\n192\t#         disabled so grep no-match is safe; double-defense pattern)\n193\t#\n194\t# Plus existing skip rules (preserved):"}]},"uuid":"cd62b940-7910-46ce-b270-263d0d37efac","timestamp":"2026-05-06T13:50:58.257Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\bash-hook-lint.sh","content":"fi\n\n# === Check 6: L-S48m-1 CLAUDE_SESSION_ID empty-on-Windows anti-pattern (Pattern B) ===\n# Origin: S48m profile-template-auto-populate.sh — marker filename used $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID\n# without :- fallback; on Windows the env var is empty → marker collides on every fire →\n# silent skip after first fire. L-S48m-1 fix: use session-log basename instead.\nif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n    BAD_B=\"$(grep -nE \"[\\\"]?\\\\.[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+(-fired|-marker|-ran|-flag)-\\\\\\$\\\\{?CLAUDE_SESSION_ID\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -vE \"^[[:space:]]*[0-9]+:[[:space:]]*#\" || true)\"\n    if [ -n \"$BAD_B\" ]; then\n      emit \"L-S48m-1-CLAUDE-SESSION-ID-MARKER\" \"$bn\" \"marker filename uses \\$CLAUDE_SESSION_ID (empty on Windows; silent skip — L-S48m-1)\"\n    fi\n  done\nfi\n\n# === Check 7: L-S48d-1 pipefail+ERR-trap+bare-grep anti-pattern (Pattern C) — REFINED S58 ===\n# Origin: S48d profile-template-auto-populate.sh — set -uo pipefail + trap 'exit 0' ERR\n# + bare `grep -m1 ...` returning nonzero on no-match → ERR trap fires silently → script\n# exits 0 with NO log line → invisible failure. {"parentUuid":"2953cca9-2209-4dd6-b377-207bdb249294","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01APrKjqz7izpnbrAHQfDZRU","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_014XEdtofxUNqxEh3stHfcV4","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\bash-hook-lint.sh","old_string":"# === Check 6: L-S48m-1 CLAUDE_SESSION_ID empty-on-Windows anti-pattern (Pattern B) ===\n# Origin: S48m profile-template-auto-populate.sh — marker filename used $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID\n# without :- fallback; on Windows the env var is empty → marker collides on every fire →\n# silent skip after first fire. L-S48m-1 fix: use session-log basename instead.\nif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n    BAD_B=\"$(grep -nE \"[\\\"]?\\\\.[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+(-fired|-marker|-ran|-flag)-\\\\\\$\\\\{?CLAUDE_SESSION_ID\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -vE \"^[[:space:]]*[0-9]+:[[:space:]]*#\" || true)\"\n    if [ -n \"$BAD_B\" ]; then\n      emit \"L-S48m-1-CLAUDE-SESSION-ID-MARKER\" \"$bn\" \"marker filename uses \\$CLAUDE_SESSION_ID (empty on Windows; silent skip — L-S48m-1)\"\n    fi\n  done\nfi","new_string":"# === Check 6: L-S48m-1 CLAUDE_SESSION_ID empty-on-Windows anti-pattern (Pattern B) ===\n# Origin: S48m profile-template-auto-populate.sh — marker filename used $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID\n# without :- fallback; on Windows the env var is empty → marker collides on every fire →\n# silent skip after first fire. L-S48m-1 fix: use session-log basename instead.\nif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n    BAD_B=\"$(grep -nE \"[\\\"]?\\\\.[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+(-fired|-marker|-ran|-flag)-\\\\\\$\\\\{?CLAUDE_SESSION_ID\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -vE \"^[[:space:]]*[0-9]+:[[:space:]]*#\" || true)\"\n    if [ -n \"$BAD_B\" ]; then\n      emit \"L-S48m-1-CLAUDE-SESSION-ID-MARKER\" \"$bn\" \"marker filename uses \\$CLAUDE_SESSION_ID (empty on Windows; silent skip — L-S48m-1)\"\n    fi\n  done\nfi\n\n# === Check 6b: L-S108-1 CLAUDE_SESSION_ID fallback-to-constant anti-pattern (Pattern B') ===\n# Origin: S108 qa-pending-auto-mover.sh — SID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-main}\" then\n# MARKER=\"...-${SID}\". CLAUDE_SESSION_ID empty on Windows → fallback \"main\" shared across\n# ALL sessions → marker filename collides → permanent idempotency lockout (M-S108-1: bundle\n# stuck in pending/ for 24+h). Check 6 only catches DIRECT $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID in marker;\n# misses INDIRECT-via-VAR form. Fix: use date hour-bucket or session-log basename.\n#\n# Detection: file has BOTH (a) fallback-to-constant assignment AND (b) any marker filename\n# pattern using $VAR. {"parentUuid":"5dad9117-ad46-40ec-b652-c1246bb93a54","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"39f27084-b50e-473e-be26-800a00cbc827","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Wpg2kBi1eMTeDLppySW6wc","type":"tool_result","content":"The file C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\qa-pending-auto-mover.sh has been updated successfully."}]},"uuid":"33cb5d67-2527-494c-9ad2-5562b14d71f3","timestamp":"2026-05-06T13:55:21.637Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\qa-pending-auto-mover.sh","oldString":"MEM_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.qa-auto-mv.log\"\nSID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-main}\"\n# Marker uses HOUR-BUCKET (NOT $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID-with-fallback per L-S108-1).","newString":"MEM_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.qa-auto-mv.log\"\n# Marker uses HOUR-BUCKET (NOT $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID-with-fallback per L-S108-1).","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# qa-pending-auto-mover.sh — HH-E.2 companion deliverable (Phase 2.5 harness hardening).\n#\n# Stop hook scans human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/ for bundles whose frontmatter\n# `status:` starts with `answered-|closed-|resolved-` AND whose `wait_until:` (if\n# present) ISO-8601 timestamp is in the past, AND no global `.auto-mv-paused` kill\n# switch exists. Mv qualifying bundles to human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/.\n# Idempotent per hour-bucket via marker file (L-S108-1: per-session intent\n# requires per-session signal; CLAUDE_SESSION_ID empty on Windows so we use\n# date hour-bucket instead).\n#\n# Bash + POSIX only per L-S11-1. Pipefail-bracket pattern per L-S48d-1.\n# Stop hook (priority: late chain — AFTER qa-stale-urgent-escalator.sh).\n# Authorized by D-031 (charter ratification 2026-05-05) — agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md\n# § \"Connection to human-workspace/\" Auto-mv rule.\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\n# === L-S48d-1 lint flag — S57 ratify per KI-S54-1 (alt-guard recognition) ===\n# bash-hook-lint Check 7 flags this file. Pattern: explicit `set +o pipefail\n# / grep / set -o pipefail` brackets around each grep, PLUS `|| echo \"\"` /\n# `|| echo 0` end-of-pipeline alt-guard.\n#   `STATUS_LINE=$(head -25 ... | grep -m1 '^status:' || echo \"\")` — alt-guard ✓\n#   `WAIT_LINE=$(head -25 ... | grep -m1 '^wait_until:' || echo \"\")` — alt-guard ✓\n#   `WAIT_EPOCH=$(date -d \"$WAIT_VAL\" +%s 2>/dev/null || echo 0)` — alt-guard ✓\n# Pipefail-bracket double-defense; all grep usages SAFE. Lint refinement\n# to recognize pipefail-bracket + `|| echo NN` deferred per KI-S54-1.\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nQA_PEND=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/pending\"\nQA_ANS=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered\"\nKILL_SWITCH=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/.auto-mv-paused\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.qa-auto-mv.log\"\nSID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-main}\"\n# Marker uses HOUR-BUCKET (NOT $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID-with-fallback per L-S108-1).\n# Reason: $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID empty on Windows → fallback-to-constant (\"main\")\n# shares marker across all sessions → permanent idempotency lockout (M-S108-1\n# RCA: bundle stuck in pending/ for 24+h before discovery). Hour-bucket\n# self-recovers (next hour creates fresh marker) and works on Windows.\nBUCKET=\"$(date +%Y%m%d-%H 2>/dev/null)\"\n[ -z \"$BUCKET\" ] && BUCKET=\"unbucketed-$$\"  # PID fallback if date fails\nMARKER=\"$MEM_DIR/.qa-auto-mv-fired-${BUCKET}\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\nNOW_EPOCH=\"$(date +%s 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n\n# Cleanup stale markers from prior buckets (L-S108-1 prevention).\n# Keeps current bucket marker; removes all others matching pattern.\nfor old_marker in \"$MEM_DIR\"/.qa-auto-mv-fired-*; do\n  [ -f \"$old_marker\" ] || continue  # glob no-match safety\n  case \"$old_marker\" in\n    *\".qa-auto-mv-fired-${BUCKET}\") ;;  # current bucket → keep\n    *) rm -f \"$old_marker\" 2>/dev/null ;;  # stale → delete\n  esac\ndone\n\n# Idempotency: skip if already fired this hour-bucket\n[ -f \"$MARKER\" ] && exit 0\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\" \"$QA_ANS\"\n\n# Skip if pending dir absent\n[ -d \"$QA_PEND\" ] || { echo \"fired\" > \"$MARKER\"; exit 0; }\n\n# Global kill switch\nif [ -f \"$KILL_SWITCH\" ]; then\n  printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: kill switch %s present; skip all mv\\n' \"$TS\" \"$KILL_SWITCH\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  echo \"fired\" > \"$MARKER\"\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Iterate pending bundles\nMOVED=0\nSKIPPED=0\nDEFERRED=0\n\nset +o pipefail\nPENDING_FILES=$(find \"$QA_PEND\" -maxdepth 1 -type f -name \"*.md\" 2>/dev/null | sort)\nset -o pipefail\n\n[ -z \"$PENDING_FILES\" ] && { echo \"fired\" > \"$MARKER\"; exit 0; }\n\nwhile IFS= read -r f; do\n  [ -z \"$f\" ] && continue\n  base=$(basename \"$f\")\n\n  # Condition 1: status field starts with answered-|closed-|resolved-\n  set +o pipefail\n  STATUS_LINE=$(head -25 \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -m1 '^status:' || echo \"\")\n  set -o pipefail\n\n  if [ -z \"$STATUS_LINE\" ]; then\n    SKIPPED=$((SKIPPED + 1))\n    printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: SKIP %s (no status: field in head -25)\\n' \"$TS\" \"$base\" >> \"$LOG\"\n    continue\n  fi\n\n  # Strip \"status:\" prefix + leading whitespace; lowercase compare\n  STATUS_VAL=$(printf '%s' \"$STATUS_LINE\" | sed 's/^status:[[:space:]]*//' | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')\n\n  case \"$STATUS_VAL\" in\n    answered-*|closed-*|resolved-*) ;;  # match → continue to condition 2\n    *)\n      SKIPPED=$((SKIPPED + 1))\n      printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: SKIP %s (status not answered-/closed-/resolved-: %s)\\n' \"$TS\" \"$base\" \"$STATUS_VAL\" >> \"$LOG\"\n      continue\n      ;;\n  esac\n\n  # Condition 2: wait_until ISO timestamp; if present AND > NOW → defer\n  set +o pipefail\n  WAIT_LINE=$(head -25 \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -m1 '^wait_until:' || echo \"\")\n  set -o pipefail\n\n  if [ -n \"$WAIT_LINE\" ]; then\n    WAIT_VAL=$(printf '%s' \"$WAIT_LINE\" | sed 's/^wait_until:[[:space:]]*//' | sed 's/[[:space:]]*$//')\n    # Parse ISO via date -d if available; on failure, treat as past (allow mv)\n    set +o pipefail\n    WAIT_EPOCH=$(date -d \"$WAIT_VAL\" +%s 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n    set -o pipefail\n    if [ \"$WAIT_EPOCH\" -gt \"$NOW_EPOCH\" ]; then\n      DEFERRED=$((DEFERRED + 1))\n      printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: DEFER %s (wait_until=%s > NOW)\\n' \"$TS\" \"$base\" \"$WAIT_VAL\" >> \"$LOG\"\n      continue\n    fi\n  fi\n\n  # All conditions hold → mv\n  if mv \"$f\" \"$QA_ANS/$base\" 2>>\"$LOG\"; then\n    MOVED=$((MOVED + 1))\n    printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: MV %s → answered/ (status=%s)\\n' \"$TS\" \"$base\" \"$STATUS_VAL\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  else\n    SKIPPED=$((SKIPPED + 1))\n    printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: MV-FAIL %s (status=%s)\\n' \"$TS\" \"$base\" \"$STATUS_VAL\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  fi\ndone <<< \"$PENDING_FILES\"\n\n# Summary log + idempotency marker (hour-bucketed per L-S108-1)\nprintf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: session=%s bucket=%s moved=%d deferred=%d skipped=%d\\n' \"$TS\" \"$SID\" \"$BUCKET\" \"$MOVED\" \"$DEFERRED\" \"$SKIPPED\" >> \"$LOG\"\necho \"fired\" > \"$MARKER\"\n\n# Soft-warn stderr only when non-zero mv\nif [ \"$MOVED\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  echo \"qa-pending-auto-mover: auto-mv'd $MOVED bundle(s) pending/ → answered/ (deferred=$DEFERRED skipped=$SKIPPED)\" >&2\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":33,"oldLines":7,"newStart":33,"newLines":6,"lines":[" KILL_SWITCH=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/.auto-mv-paused\""," MEM_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\""," LOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.qa-auto-mv.log\"","-SID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-main}\""," # Marker uses HOUR-BUCKET (NOT $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID-with-fallback per L-S108-1)."," # Reason: $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID empty on Windows → fallback-to-constant (\"main\")"," # shares marker across all sessions → permanent idempotency lockout (M-S108-1"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5dad9117-ad46-40ec-b652-c1246bb93a54","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"a66c838a-2292-4d0c-8968-e81af473cb00","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Mv qualifying bundles to human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/.\n# Idempotent per hour-bucket via marker file (L-S108-1: per-session intent\n# requires per-session signal; CLAUDE_SESSION_ID empty on Windows so we use\n# date hour-bucket instead).\n#\n# Bash + POSIX only per L-S11-1. Pipefail-bracket pattern per L-S48d-1.\n# Stop hook (priority: late chain — AFTER qa-stale-urgent-escalator.sh).\n# Authorized by D-031 (charter ratification 2026-05-05) — agent-workspace/CLAUDE.md\n# § \"Connection to human-workspace/\" Auto-mv rule.\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\n# === L-S48d-1 lint flag — S57 ratify per KI-S54-1 (alt-guard recognition) ===\n# bash-hook-lint Check 7 flags this file. Pattern: explicit `set +o pipefail\n# / grep / set -o pipefail` brackets around each grep, PLUS `|| echo \"\"` /\n# `|| echo 0` end-of-pipeline alt-guard.\n#   `STATUS_LINE=$(head -25 ... | grep -m1 '^status:' || echo \"\")` — alt-guard ✓\n#   `WAIT_LINE=$(head -25 ... | grep -m1 '^wait_until:' || echo \"\")` — alt-guard ✓\n#   `WAIT_EPOCH=$(date -d \"$WAIT_VAL\" +%s 2>/dev/null || echo 0)` — alt-guard ✓\n# Pipefail-bracket double-defense; all grep usages SAFE. Lint refinement\n# to recognize pipefail-bracket + `|| echo NN` deferred per KI-S54-1.\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nQA_PEND=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/pending\"\nQA_ANS=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/answered\"\nKILL_SWITCH=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/q-and-a/.auto-mv-paused\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.qa-auto-mv.log\"\n# Marker uses HOUR-BUCKET (NOT $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID-with-fallback per L-S108-1).\n# Reason: $CLAUDE_SESSION_ID empty on Windows → fallback-to-constant (\"main\")\n# shares marker across all sessions → permanent idempotency lockout (M-S108-1\n# RCA: bundle stuck in pending/ for 24+h before discovery). Hour-bucket\n# self-recovers (next hour creates fresh marker) and works on Windows.\nBUCKET=\"$(date +%Y%m%d-%H 2>/dev/null)\"\n[ -z \"$BUCKET\" ] && BUCKET=\"unbucketed-$$\"  # PID fallback if date fails\nMARKER=\"$MEM_DIR/.qa-auto-mv-fired-${BUCKET}\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\nNOW_EPOCH=\"$(date +%s 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n\n# Cleanup stale markers from prior buckets (L-S108-1 prevention).\n# Keeps current bucket marker; removes all others matching pattern.\nfor old_marker in \"$MEM_DIR\"/.qa-auto-mv-fired-*; do\n  [ -f \"$old_marker\" ] || continue  # glob no-match safety\n  case \"$old_marker\" in\n    *\".qa-auto-mv-fired-${BUCKET}\") ;;  # current bucket → keep\n    *) rm -f \"$old_marker\" 2>/dev/null ;;  # stale → delete\n  esac\ndone\n\n# Idempotency: skip if already fired this hour-bucket\n[ -f \"$MARKER\" ] && exit 0\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\" \"$QA_ANS\"\n\n# Skip if pending dir absent\n[ -d \"$QA_PEND\" ] || { echo \"fired\" > \"$MARKER\"; exit 0; }\n\n# Global kill switch\nif [ -f \"$KILL_SWITCH\" ]; then\n  printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: kill switch %s present; skip all mv\\n' \"$TS\" \"$KILL_SWITCH\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  echo \"fired\" > \"$MARKER\"\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Iterate pending bundles\nMOVED=0\nSKIPPED=0\nDEFERRED=0\n\nset +o pipefail\nPENDING_FILES=$(find \"$QA_PEND\" -maxdepth 1 -type f -name \"*.md\" 2>/dev/null | sort)\nset -o pipefail\n\n[ -z \"$PENDING_FILES\" ] && { echo \"fired\" > \"$MARKER\"; exit 0; }\n\nwhile IFS= read -r f; do\n  [ -z \"$f\" ] && continue\n  base=$(basename \"$f\")\n\n  # Condition 1: status field starts with answered-|closed-|resolved-\n  set +o pipefail\n  STATUS_LINE=$(head -25 \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -m1 '^status:' || echo \"\")\n  set -o pipefail\n\n  if [ -z \"$STATUS_LINE\" ]; then\n    SKIPPED=$((SKIPPED + 1))\n    printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: SKIP %s (no status: field in head -25)\\n' \"$TS\" \"$base\" >> \"$LOG\"\n    continue\n  fi\n\n  # Strip \"status:\" prefix + leading whitespace; lowercase compare\n  STATUS_VAL=$(printf '%s' \"$STATUS_LINE\" | sed 's/^status:[[:space:]]*//' | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')\n\n  case \"$STATUS_VAL\" in\n    answered-*|closed-*|resolved-*) ;;  # match → continue to condition 2\n    *)\n      SKIPPED=$((SKIPPED + 1))\n      printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: SKIP %s (status not answered-/closed-/resolved-: %s)\\n' \"$TS\" \"$base\" \"$STATUS_VAL\" >> \"$LOG\"\n      continue\n      ;;\n  esac\n\n  # Condition 2: wait_until ISO timestamp; if present AND > NOW → defer\n  set +o pipefail\n  WAIT_LINE=$(head -25 \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -m1 '^wait_until:' || echo \"\")\n  set -o pipefail\n\n  if [ -n \"$WAIT_LINE\" ]; then\n    WAIT_VAL=$(printf '%s' \"$WAIT_LINE\" | sed 's/^wait_until:[[:space:]]*//' | sed 's/[[:space:]]*$//')\n    # Parse ISO via date -d if available; on failure, treat as past (allow mv)\n    set +o pipefail\n    WAIT_EPOCH=$(date -d \"$WAIT_VAL\" +%s 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n    set -o pipefail\n    if [ \"$WAIT_EPOCH\" -gt \"$NOW_EPOCH\" ]; then\n      DEFERRED=$((DEFERRED + 1))\n      printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: DEFER %s (wait_until=%s > NOW)\\n' \"$TS\" \"$base\" \"$WAIT_VAL\" >> \"$LOG\"\n      continue\n    fi\n  fi\n\n  # All conditions hold → mv\n  if mv \"$f\" \"$QA_ANS/$base\" 2>>\"$LOG\"; then\n    MOVED=$((MOVED + 1))\n    printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: MV %s → answered/ (status=%s)\\n' \"$TS\" \"$base\" \"$STATUS_VAL\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  else\n    SKIPPED=$((SKIPPED + 1))\n    printf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: MV-FAIL %s (status=%s)\\n' \"$TS\" \"$base\" \"$STATUS_VAL\" >> \"$LOG\"\n  fi\ndone <<< \"$PENDING_FILES\"\n\n# Summary log + idempotency marker (hour-bucketed per L-S108-1)\nprintf '[%s] qa-pending-auto-mover: session=%s bucket=%s moved=%d deferred=%d skipped=%d\\n' \"$TS\" \"$SID\" \"$BUCKET\" \"$MOVED\" \"$DEFERRED\" \"$SKIPPED\" >> \"$LOG\"\necho \"fired\" > \"$MARKER\"\n\n# Soft-warn stderr only when non-zero mv\nif [ \"$MOVED\" -gt 0 ];
